# Time log

Wall-clock statistics for the DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash A3 W8A8 implementation
task, per the launcher's request. Every work block gets one row with real
`date +%s` timestamps; durations are computed from them. Append-only; keep
historical rows even when superseded.

- Goal start epoch: 1793617630 (2026-11-02 local)
- Launcher/observer: the outer Claude Code session (spec capture,
  environment verification, checkpoint verification, and feasibility were
  done before goal start; see TASKS.md evidence).
- Implementation agent: launched model Main Agent, delegated 2026-11-02
  (fresh session; owns implementation and acceptance per PROMPT.md).

## Categories

| Key | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| launch-spec | specification, inspection, user agreement |
| env-prep | environment/toolchain preparation and verification |
| study | reading rules, docs, prior art, harness APIs |
| dev-code | writing/editing kernels, goldens, validation code |
| compile | pypto compilation / PTOAS artifacts |
| validate-sim | golden validation and structural runs (simulator) |
| device-run | NPU execution (including queue wait) |
| reference-prep | independent references, fixture calibration |
| weight-ops | weight mapping, loading, conversion |
| debug-fix | failure analysis, repair, reruns |
| docs-report | evidence, TASKS updates, documentation, reporting |
